2026-10-19T08:52:55.6783191Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Serve the Prefill overlay with per-128 FC2 activation scales and the FC2 staging swizzle in MegaMoE, without a build

Same configuration as a896832 (image 317ab7c7 from 2d294f0, SGLang overlay
add-rmsnorm-group-quant + prefill-attention-contiguous-kv), plus a DeepGEMM
header overlay (main 9861336) for the Prefill MegaMoE kernel:

- patches/deep-gemm-overlay/sm90-prefill-fc2-sf128.patch: FC2 input
  activations (SwiGLU output) quantized with one FP32 scale per token and
  128 channels instead of 64, the granularity the checkpoint format implies
  (weight_block_size [128, 128], activation_scheme dynamic) and SGLang's
  standard FP8 group; approved by the user. FP8 e4m3 and FP32 accumulation
  are unchanged. FC2's K loop then issues one WGMMA group per K block with
  one rescale and one wait, like FC1, instead of two groups with a full
  drain between them.
- patches/deep-gemm-overlay/sm90-prefill-fc2-swizzle.patch: XOR swizzle of
  the FC2 BF16 staging rows (8-way bank conflict on every store before).

Kernel bench 39217809231 (981c3a0, 4 GPUs, a8ed4592 headers, same DeepGEMM
series as 317ab7c7): gate passed; prefill-balanced-4096 1.583 -> 1.566 ms
(-1.0%), prefill-local-4096 1.461 -> 1.388 ms (-5.0%); timeline per CTA:
FC2 K loop 485 -> 391 us (342 us without the swizzle), FC2 MMA drain wait
59 -> 8 us, kernel end 1758 -> 1548 us, but the FC2 NVLink scatter grew
79 -> 163 us: with faster FC2 math the combine traffic (about 200 MB of
BF16 per rank) bounds the FC2 phase at the 1.9 GHz bench clock. Serving runs
the Prefill GPUs at about 1.47 GHz under the 700 W cap, where the math share
is larger, so this run measures what remains there.

The numerical gate now uses a per-128 reference for Prefill-geometry cases
(bounds unchanged), as in the kernel bench. Decode (SwapAB) is unaffected:
its instantiation and SASS are identical.
